# Skills · P · part 17

806 entries, paper-watch to paperctl. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **paper-watch**: Show the newest arXiv research papers on a topic that the user has NOT seen before, by running this skill's bundled script — never from memory. The script reads progress.md (the spine) first to learn what was already shown, fetches the latest papers from arXiv, prints only the genuinely new ones, and writes progress.md last. Use it whenever the user asks what is new on arXiv, wants new papers or fresh research on a topic, wants a paper feed, or runs a scheduled "what's new since yesterday" job. Only new-since-last-time counts — showing a paper twice defeats the point of a watch. ([panaversity/agentfactory-labs](https://github.com/panaversity/agentfactory-labs/tree/HEAD/crash-course/loop-eng/paper-watch/.claude/skills/paper-watch) · ★11 · 2 copies)
